Shin Megami Tensei IV

Spoiler-free 100% completion for the original Nintendo 3DS release · 37 areas · 1084 tracked items

This page shows everything, including exact locations. It exists so you can search for one specific collectible and find it. If you are playing blind, use the interactive spoiler-free version instead, it hides locations until you ask for them, one item at a time.

Gather Some Naraku Lichen (missable)

Challenge Quest record

HintRefresh tavern scoreboards and speak to peaceful contacts after each main assignment.
WhereAccept Gather Some Naraku Lichen from K, then pin it in the Gauntlet log.
Full detailWith Gather Some Naraku Lichen active, complete this objective: Collect Deep Green Moss x3. Return to K whenever a hand-in is required, confirm the completed mark, and verify receipt of Holy Fruit x3; holding the requested object without turning it in does not close the quest.

Recovery Catalogue

You are here when: The recovery inventory tab, its associated shop lists, and quest-reward screen.

Only player-controlled navigation and completion states are exposed; narrative identities and events remain hidden.

Melee Catalogue

You are here when: The melee inventory tab, its associated shop lists, and quest-reward screen.

Only player-controlled navigation and completion states are exposed; narrative identities and events remain hidden.

Head Catalogue

You are here when: The head inventory tab, its associated shop lists, and quest-reward screen.

Only player-controlled navigation and completion states are exposed; narrative identities and events remain hidden.

Body Catalogue

You are here when: The body inventory tab, its associated shop lists, and quest-reward screen.

Only player-controlled navigation and completion states are exposed; narrative identities and events remain hidden.
